    On Oct. 26, the UK Maritime Trade Operations Centre reported
a tanker exiting Hormuz was disabled after being struck by an
unknown projectile Oct. 23. No crew casualties or oil spills have
been reported.

    Of the 16 VLCC crossings identified over Oct. 23-25, 10 were
assessed as inbound and six outbound, CAS said. Inbound VLCC
movements included five of South Korea's Sinokor-operated
ships, and the Saudi-linked Majra and Qasba. The VLCC Ennie
moved into the Persian Gulf after last loading a cargo of Basrah
Heavy crude in early October, and China-owned VLCC Indigo Ray
also moved inbound, CAS said.

    Outbound transits included Sinokor-operated Romania
Prosperity, UAE-linked Das and Al Faya, and Saudi-linked Kahla
and Emerald Shine, CAS said.

Freight costs remain elevated at 255/mt

    Water levels on the Rhine, a critical European inland waterway
for freight, rose above 50 centimeters Oct. 26 and are forecast
to hover around 70 cm in the next few days, but the rebound in
flows is expected to provide only short-term relief to fuel supply
chains across the region, according to market participants.

    Water levels at Kaub, the shallowest part of the Middle Rhine,
stood at 56 cm as of late afternoon Oct. 26 and are forecast
to exceed 70 cm by the end of the day, then expected to hold
around that level for the following three days, according to data
from the German water authority WSV.

    The Rhine water levels exceeded 50 cm on Oct. 26 for the first
time since Sept. 4, with levels mostly below 10 cm from Sept. 24
to Oct. 10 and mostly below 20 cm from Oct. 11 to Oct. 22.

    The recent rise in Rhine water levels has generated renewed

BFF PFEEQ00 lhc  2           QR EUR CPA 96 Grade DAP Central Europe                   (100%) alcohol. One HLTPA equals 1 hectoliter (100 liters) of pure

                                                   Eur/CPA Contract Qtly              alcohol. Unlike a standard hectoliter (hL), which measures the

                                                   Platts FOLicht Ethanol Industrial  total volume of any liquid, HLTPA refers only to the pure alcohol

                                                   Eur/CPA Contract Qtly              content, regardless of the concentration in the original beverage.
